--- RAG-CDB ---
What are the five commonly taken specimens for patients who received extirpation of matter from left lower lung lobe, via natural or artificial opening endoscopic previously during the same month since 2100?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.spec_type_desc FROM ( SELECT T2.spec_type_desc, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'extirpation of matter from left lower lung lobe, via natural or artificial opening endoscopic' ) AND strftime('%Y-%m',procedures_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y-%m',current_time) ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc, microbiologyevents.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM microbiologyevents JOIN admissions ON microbiologyevents.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id GROUP BY T2.spec_type_desc ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'microbiologyevents.charttime', 'microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't2.spec_type_desc', 't3.spec_type_desc'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_procedures', 'microbiologyevents', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'extirpation of matter from left lower lung lobe, via natural or artificial opening endoscopic'], ["strftime('%y-%m',procedures_icd.charttime)", '=', "strftime('%y-%m','2100-12-31 23:59:00')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.spec_type_desc FROM ( SELECT T2.spec_type_desc, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'extirpation of matter from left lower lung lobe, via natural or artificial opening endoscopic' ) AND strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc, microbiologyevents.charttime FROM microbiologyevents JOIN admissions ON microbiologyevents.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE strftime('%Y',microbiologyevents.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month') GROUP BY T2.spec_type_desc ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'microbiologyevents.charttime', 'microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't2.spec_type_desc', 't3.spec_type_desc'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_procedures', 'microbiologyevents', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'extirpation of matter from left lower lung lobe, via natural or artificial opening endoscopic'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.charttime,'start of month')"], ["strftime('%y',microbiologyevents.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}

How many patients in 2100 received central venous catheter placement with guidance after the reopening of recent thoracotomy site procedure within the same month?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT T1.subject_id) FROM ( SELECT procedures_icd.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'reopening of recent thoracotomy site' ) AND strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT procedures_icd.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'central venous catheter placement with guidance' ) AND strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['d_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.subject_id', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['d_icd_procedures', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'central venous catheter placement with guidance'], ['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'reopening of recent thoracotomy site'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.charttime,'start of month')"], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'reopening of recent thoracotomy site' ) AND strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'central venous catheter placement with guidance' ) AND strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_procedures', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'central venous catheter placement with guidance'], ['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'reopening of recent thoracotomy site'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.charttime,'start of month')"], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime']]}

In 2100, how many patients received pantoprazole in the same hospital encounter after being diagnosed with defibrination syndrome?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT admissions.subject_id) 
FROM admissions 
WHERE admissions.hadm_id IN (
    SELECT diagnoses_icd.hadm_id 
    FROM diagnoses_icd 
    WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
        WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'defibrination syndrome'
    ) 
    AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100'
) 
AND admissions.hadm_id IN (
    SELECT prescriptions.hadm_id 
    FROM prescriptions 
    WHERE prescriptions.drug = 'pantoprazole' 
    AND strftime('%Y', prescriptions.starttime) = '2100'
)
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.hadm_id', 'prescriptions.hadm_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'defibrination syndrome'], ['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'pantoprazole'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',prescriptions.starttime)", '=', '2100']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'defibrination syndrome' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.starttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE prescriptions.drug = 'pantoprazole' AND strftime('%Y',prescriptions.starttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'defibrination syndrome'], ['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'pantoprazole'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',prescriptions.starttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id']]}
----------

Count the number of patients diagnosed with gastro-esophageal reflux disease without esophagitis who did not come to the hospital within 2 months since 2100.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT diagnoses_icd.subject_id) 
FROM diagnoses_icd 
JOIN d_icd_diagnoses ON diagnoses_icd.icd_code = d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'gastro-esophageal reflux disease without esophagitis' 
AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100' 
AND diagnoses_icd.subject_id NOT IN (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id 
    FROM admissions 
    WHERE datetime(admissions.admittime) BETWEEN datetime('2100-01-01') AND datetime('2100-03-01')
)
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'diagnoses_icd.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'gastro-esophageal reflux disease without esophagitis'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT ( SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'gastro-esophageal reflux disease without esophagitis' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T1 ) - ( SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T2.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'gastro-esophageal reflux disease without esophagitis' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T2 JOIN admissions ON T2.subject_id = admissions.subject_id WHERE T2.charttime < admissions.admittime AND strftime('%Y',admissions.admittime) >= '2100' AND datetime(admissions.admittime) BETWEEN datetime(T2.charttime) AND datetime(T2.charttime,'+2 month') )
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id', 't2.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'gastro-esophageal reflux disease without esophagitis'], ["strftime('%y',admissions.admittime)", '>=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ['t2.charttime', '<', 'admissions.admittime']]}
----------

Among patients who were diagnosed with acute alcoholic intoxication in alcoholism, continuous during this year, what are the top five most commonly prescribed medications that followed in the same hospital encounter for patients in their 50s?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'acute alcoholic intoxication in alcoholism, continuous' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y',current_time) ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 50 AND 59 AND strftime('%Y',prescriptions.starttime) = strftime('%Y',current_time) ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'acute alcoholic intoxication in alcoholism, continuous'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', "strftime('%y','2100-12-31 23:59:00')"], ["strftime('%y',prescriptions.starttime)", '=', "strftime('%y','2100-12-31 23:59:00')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'acute alcoholic intoxication in alcoholism, continuous' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 50 AND 59 AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'acute alcoholic intoxication in alcoholism, continuous'], ["datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ["datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}

How many patients were prescribed the medicine metronidazole (flagyl) within 2 months after a diagnosis of urinary tract infection, site not specified since 1 year ago?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT admissions.subject_id) 
FROM admissions 
WHERE admissions.hadm_id IN (
    SELECT prescriptions.hadm_id 
    FROM prescriptions 
    WHERE prescriptions.drug = 'metronidazole (flagyl)' 
    AND prescriptions.starttime BETWEEN (
        SELECT diagnoses_icd.charttime 
        FROM diagnoses_icd 
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'urinary tract infection, site not specified'
        ) 
        AND diagnoses_icd.charttime >= datetime(current_time, '-1 year')
    ) 
    AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime, '+2 months')
)
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.hadm_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'urinary tract infection, site not specified'], ['diagnoses_icd.charttime', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', '-1 year')"], ['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'metronidazole (flagyl)']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'urinary tract infection, site not specified' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE prescriptions.drug = 'metronidazole (flagyl)' AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T2.starttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime,'+2 month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'urinary tract infection, site not specified'], ['datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year')"], ['datetime(prescriptions.starttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year')"], ['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'metronidazole (flagyl)'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime']]}

What were the top three most frequent microbiology tests that patients were given after being diagnosed with acquired absence of organ, genital organs during the same hospital encounter since 2100?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.test_name FROM (
    SELECT T2.test_name, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id
        FROM diagnoses_icd
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'acquired absence of organ, genital organs'
        )
        AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime('2100-01-01')
    ) AS T1
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, microbiologyevents.test_name, microbiologyevents.charttime, admissions.hadm_id
        FROM microbiologyevents
        JOIN admissions ON microbiologyevents.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE datetime(microbiologyevents.charttime) >= datetime('2100-01-01')
    ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id
    GROUP BY T2.test_name
) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 3
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'microbiologyevents.charttime', 'microbiologyevents.test_name', 't2.test_name', 't3.test_name'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'microbiologyevents'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'acquired absence of organ, genital organs'], ['datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-01-01')"], ['datetime(microbiologyevents.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-01-01')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '3']]}
----------
TRUTH:

What are the four most commonly prescribed drugs for patients who were diagnosed with urinary incontinence, unspecified previously within the same month?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM (
    SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1 
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
        FROM diagnoses_icd 
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'urinary incontinence, unspecified'
        )
    ) AS T1 
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime 
        FROM prescriptions 
        JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
    ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id 
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime 
    AND datetime(T1.charttime, 'start of month') = datetime(T2.starttime, 'start of month') 
    GROUP BY T2.drug
) AS T3 
WHERE T3.C1 <= 4

What are the three most frequently ordered medications that female patients aged 60 or above were prescribed within 2 months after they were diagnosed with unspecified essential hypertension in 2100?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM (
    SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime
        FROM diagnoses_icd
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        JOIN patients ON admissions.subject_id = patients.subject_id
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'unspecified essential hypertension'
        )
        AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100'
        AND patients.gender = 'f'
        AND admissions.age >= 60
    ) AS T1
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime
        FROM prescriptions
        JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE strftime('%Y', prescriptions.starttime) = '2100'
    ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime
    AND datetime(T2.starttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime, '+2 month')
    GROUP BY T2.drug
) AS T3
WHERE T3.C1 <= 3
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['admissions.age', '>=', '60'], ['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'unspecified essential hypertension'], ['patients.gender', '=', 'f'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',prescriptions.starttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '3']]}
----------
TRUTH:

    FROM prescriptions
    WHERE prescriptions.hadm_id IN (

        FROM prescriptions
        WHERE prescriptions.drug = 'fentanyl citrate'
        AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime, 'start of year') = datetime(current_time, 'start of year', '-0 year')
    )
    AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime, 'start of year') = datetime(current_time, 'start of year', '-0 year')
    GROUP BY prescriptions.drug
) AS T1 WHERE T1.C1 <= 5

How many people received a insertion or replacement of external ventricular drain [evd] procedure within 2 months after they had been diagnosed with methicillin resistant pneumonia due to staphylococcus aureus since 2100?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT admissions.subject_id) 
FROM admissions 
WHERE admissions.hadm_id IN (
    SELECT procedures_icd.hadm_id 
    FROM procedures_icd 
    WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_procedures 
        WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'insertion or replacement of external ventricular drain [evd]'
    ) 
    AND procedures_icd.charttime BETWEEN (
        SELECT datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime, '+2 month') 
        FROM diagnoses_icd 
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'methicillin resistant pneumonia due to staphylococcus aureus'
        ) 
        AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100'
    ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime, '+2 month')
)
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.hadm_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'd_icd_procedures', 'diagnoses_icd', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'methicillin resistant pneumonia due to staphylococcus aureus'], ['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'insertion or replacement of external ventricular drain [evd]'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'methicillin resistant pneumonia due to staphylococcus aureus' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'insertion or replacement of external ventricular drain [evd]' ) AND strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T2.charttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime,'+2 month')

What is the 3-month survival probability for patients living with a asymptomatic human immunodeficiency virus [hiv] infection status diagnosis?
----------
PREDS:
None
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['test."nothing"', '=', 'none']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT AVG(C1) FROM ( SELECT ( CASE WHEN (strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',T1.charttime)) < 1 * 365/4 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'asymptomatic human immunodeficiency virus [hiv] infection status' ) AND strftime('%J',current_time) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 1 * 365/4 GROUP BY admissions.subject_id HAVING MIN(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) AS T1 JOIN patients ON T1.subject_id = patients.subject_id )
Flattened: {'sel': 'avg', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 't1.charttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'asymptomatic human immunodeficiency virus [hiv] infection status'], ["strftime('%j','2100-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '1 * 365/4 ) = diagnoses_icd.charttime']]}

How many patients underwent reopening of recent thoracotomy site within the same month after the diagnosis with other acute postoperative pain, since 2100?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT T1.subject_id) 
FROM (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
    FROM diagnoses_icd 
    JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
        WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'other acute postoperative pain'
    ) 
    AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100'
) AS T1 
JOIN (
    SELECT procedures_icd.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime 
    FROM procedures_icd 
    WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_procedures 
        WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'reopening of recent thoracotomy site'
    ) 
    AND strftime('%Y', procedures_icd.charttime) >= '2100'
) AS T2 
ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id 
WHERE strftime('%Y-%m', T1.charttime) = strftime('%Y-%m', T2.charttime)
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.subject_id', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'd_icd_procedures', 'diagnoses_icd', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'other acute postoperative pain'], ['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'reopening of recent thoracotomy site'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y-%m',t1.charttime)", '=', "strftime('%y-%m', t2.charttime)"]]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'other acute postoperative pain' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'reopening of recent thoracotomy site' ) AND strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'd_icd_procedures', 'diagnoses_icd', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'other acute postoperative pain'], ['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'reopening of recent thoracotomy site'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.charttime,'start of month')"], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime']]}

What are the four most frequent drugs that were prescribed to female patients in their 50s during the same hospital encounter after they were diagnosed with unspecified essential hypertension in this year?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM (
    SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id
        FROM diagnoses_icd
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        JOIN patients ON admissions.subject_id = patients.subject_id
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'unspecified essential hypertension'
        )
        AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y', 'now')
        AND patients.gender = 'f'
        AND admissions.age BETWEEN 50 AND 59
    ) AS T1
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime, admissions.hadm_id
        FROM prescriptions
        JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE strftime('%Y', prescriptions.starttime) = strftime('%Y', 'now')
    ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime
    GROUP BY T2.drug
) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'unspecified essential hypertension'], ['patients.gender', '=', 'f'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', "strftime('%y', '2100-12-31 23:59:00')"], ["strftime('%y',prescriptions.starttime)", '=', "strftime('%y', '2100-12-31 23:59:00')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '4']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'unspecified essential hypertension' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE admissions.subject_id IN ( SELECT patients.subject_id FROM patients WHERE patients.gender = 'f' ) AND admissions.age BETWEEN 50 AND 59 AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.subject_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'unspecified essential hypertension'], ["datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ["datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ['patients.gender', '=', 'f'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '4']]}

PREDS:
None
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['test."nothing"', '=', 'none']]}
----------

List the top three most frequent diagnoses that were given to patients within the same month after they were diagnosed with other dependence on machines, supplemental oxygen.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.long_title 
FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code IN (
    SELECT T3.icd_code 
    FROM (
        SELECT T2.icd_code, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1 
        FROM (
            SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
            FROM diagnoses_icd 
            JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
            WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
                SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
                FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
                WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'other dependence on machines, supplemental oxygen'
            )
        ) AS T1 
        JOIN (
            SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.icd_code, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
            FROM diagnoses_icd 
            JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id 
        WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime

How many patients underwent diagnosis of postprocedural fever within 2 months after being diagnosed with body mass index 35.0-35.9, adult throughout this year?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT T1.subject_id) 
FROM (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
    FROM diagnoses_icd 
    JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
        WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'body mass index 35.0-35.9, adult'
    ) 
    AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100'
) AS T1 
JOIN (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
    FROM diagnoses_icd 
    JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
        WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'postprocedural fever'
    ) 
    AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100'
) AS T2 
ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id 
WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime 
AND datetime(T2.charttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime, '+2 month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'body mass index 35.0-35.9, adult'], ['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'postprocedural fever'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'body mass index 35.0-35.9, adult' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'postprocedural fever' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T2.charttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime,'+2 month')

Show me the top three most commonly prescribed drugs during the same month for patients in their 30s after a unspecified essential hypertension diagnosis.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM (
    SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime
        FROM diagnoses_icd
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'unspecified essential hypertension'
        )
    ) AS T1
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime
        FROM prescriptions
        JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 30 AND 39
    ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime
    AND datetime(T1.charttime, 'start of month') = datetime(T2.starttime, 'start of month')
    GROUP BY T2.drug
) AS T3
WHERE T3.C1 <= 3
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'unspecified essential hypertension'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.starttime, 'start of month')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '3']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'unspecified essential hypertension' ) ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 30 AND 39 ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.starttime,'start of month') GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 3
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'unspecified essential hypertension'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.starttime,'start of month')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '3']]}

How many patients received a closed [endoscopic] biopsy of bronchus after the procedure of arterial catheterization during the same hospital encounter?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT admissions.subject_id) 
FROM admissions 
JOIN procedures_icd AS proc1 ON admissions.hadm_id = proc1.hadm_id 
JOIN procedures_icd AS proc2 ON admissions.hadm_id = proc2.hadm_id 
WHERE proc1.icd_code = (SELECT icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE long_title = 'arterial catheterization') 
AND proc2.icd_code = (SELECT icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE long_title = 'closed [endoscopic] biopsy of bronchus') 
AND proc1.charttime < proc2.charttime;
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_procedures', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['proc1.charttime', '<', 'proc2.charttime;']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'arterial catheterization' ) ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'closed [endoscopic] biopsy of bronchus' ) ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_procedures', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'arterial catheterization'], ['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'closed [endoscopic] biopsy of bronchus'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id']]}

What is the minimum daily number of patients that are suffering from atherosclerotic heart disease of native coronary artery with unstable angina pectoris?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT MIN(daily_count) FROM (
    SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT admissions.subject_id) AS daily_count
    FROM diagnoses_icd
    JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
    WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code
        FROM d_icd_diagnoses
        WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'atherosclerotic heart disease of native coronary artery with unstable angina pectoris'
    )
    GROUP BY strftime('%Y-%m-%d', diagnoses_icd.charttime)
) AS daily_counts
Flattened: {'sel': 'min', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'atherosclerotic heart disease of native coronary artery with unstable angina pectoris']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT MIN(T1.C1) FROM ( SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT diagnoses_icd.hadm_id ) AS C1 FROM diagnoses_icd WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'atherosclerotic heart disease of native coronary artery with unstable angina pectoris' ) GROUP BY strftime('%Y-%m-%d',diagnoses_icd.charttime) ) AS T1
Flattened: {'sel': 'min', 'agg': ['d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.hadm_id'], 'tab': ['d_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'atherosclerotic heart disease of native coronary artery with unstable angina pectoris']]}

What are the three most commonly tested specimens for patients that were diagnosed with secondary diabetes mellitus without mention of complication, not stated as uncontrolled, or unspecified previously in the same month, since 1 year ago?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.spec_type_desc 
FROM (
    SELECT T2.spec_type_desc, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1 
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
        FROM diagnoses_icd 
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'secondary diabetes mellitus without mention of complication, not stated as uncontrolled, or unspecified'
        ) 
        AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime(current_time, '-1 year')
    ) AS T1 
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc, microbiologyevents.charttime 
        FROM microbiologyevents 
        JOIN admissions ON microbiologyevents.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
        WHERE datetime(microbiologyevents.charttime) >= datetime(current_time, '-1 year')
    ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id 
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime 
    AND datetime(T2.charttime, 'start of month') = datetime(T1.charttime, 'start of month') 
    GROUP BY T2.spec_type_desc
) AS T3 
WHERE T3.C1 <= 3
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'microbiologyevents.charttime', 'microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc', 't2.spec_type_desc', 't3.spec_type_desc'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'microbiologyevents'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'secondary diabetes mellitus without mention of complication, not stated as uncontrolled, or unspecified'], ['datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', '-1 year')"], ['datetime(microbiologyevents.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', '-1 year')"], ["datetime(t2.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t1.charttime, 'start of month')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '3']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.spec_type_desc FROM ( SELECT T2.spec_type_desc, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'secondary diabetes mellitus without mention of complication, not stated as uncontrolled, or unspecified' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc, microbiologyevents.charttime FROM microbiologyevents JOIN admissions ON microbiologyevents.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE datetime(microbiologyevents.charttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month') GROUP BY T2.spec_type_desc ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 3
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'microbiologyevents.charttime', 'microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc', 't2.spec_type_desc', 't3.spec_type_desc'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'microbiologyevents'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'secondary diabetes mellitus without mention of complication, not stated as uncontrolled, or unspecified'], ['datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year')"], ['datetime(microbiologyevents.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year')"], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.charttime,'start of month')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '3']]}

How much does patient 10014729 change in mch last measured on the last hospital visit compared to the second to last value measured on the last hospital visit?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT 
    (SELECT labevents.valuenum 
     FROM labevents 
     WHERE labevents.hadm_id = (
         SELECT admissions.hadm_id 
         FROM admissions 
         WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10014729 
         ORDER BY admissions.admittime DESC 
         LIMIT 1
     ) 
     AND labevents.itemid = (
         SELECT d_labitems.itemid 
         FROM d_labitems 
         WHERE d_labitems.label = 'mch'
     ) 
     ORDER BY labevents.charttime DESC 
     LIMIT 1
    ) 
    - 
    (SELECT labevents.valuenum 
     FROM labevents 
     WHERE labevents.hadm_id = (
         SELECT admissions.hadm_id 
         FROM admissions 
         WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10014729 
         ORDER BY admissions.admittime DESC 
         LIMIT 1
     ) 
     AND labevents.itemid = (
         SELECT d_labitems.itemid 
         FROM d_labitems 
         WHERE d_labitems.label = 'mch'
     ) 
     ORDER BY labevents.charttime DESC 
     LIMIT 1 OFFSET 1
    )
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'd_labitems.itemid', 'labevents.valuenum'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_labitems', 'labevents'], 'cond': [['admissions.subject_id', '=', '10014729'], ['d_labitems.label', '=', 'mch']]}
----------

What proportion of patients with a diagnosis of acetonuria survive over a 6-month period?
----------
PREDS:
None
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['test."nothing"', '=', 'none']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT AVG(C1) FROM ( SELECT ( CASE WHEN (strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',T1.charttime)) < 1 * 365/2 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'acetonuria' ) AND strftime('%J',current_time) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 1 * 365/2 GROUP BY admissions.subject_id HAVING MIN(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) AS T1 JOIN patients ON T1.subject_id = patients.subject_id )
Flattened: {'sel': 'avg', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 't1.charttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'acetonuria'], ["strftime('%j','2100-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '1 * 365/2 ) = diagnoses_icd.charttime']]}

----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT admissions.subject_id) 
FROM admissions

AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100' 
AND admissions.dischtime IS NOT NULL 
AND strftime('%Y', admissions.dischtime) = '2100' 
AND admissions.dod IS NOT NULL;
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['admissions.dischtime', 'is', 'not null'], ['admissions.dod', 'is', 'not null'], ["strftime('%y',admissions.dischtime)", '=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100']]}

----------
PREDS:
None
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['test."nothing"', '=', 'none']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'injury to thoracic aorta' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'other specified forms of effusion, except tuberculous' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id

----------

Count the number of patients who had arterial catheterization in the same month after having been diagnosed with osteoarthrosis, localized, not specified whether primary or secondary, pelvic region and thigh in 2100.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT T1.subject_id) 
FROM (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
    FROM diagnoses_icd 
    JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
        WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'osteoarthrosis, localized, not specified whether primary or secondary, pelvic region and thigh'
    ) 
    AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100'
) AS T1 
JOIN (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime 
    FROM procedures_icd 
    JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_procedures 
        WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'arterial catheterization'
    ) 
    AND strftime('%Y', procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100'
) AS T2 
ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id 
WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime 
AND datetime(T2.charttime, 'start of month') = datetime(T1.charttime, 'start of month')

Count the number of patients for whom vial was prescribed within 2 months post their diagnosis of secondary and unspecified malignant neoplasm of intra-abdominal lymph nodes in 2100.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT admissions.subject_id) 
FROM admissions 
JOIN diagnoses_icd ON admissions.hadm_id = diagnoses_icd.hadm_id 
JOIN prescriptions ON admissions.hadm_id = prescriptions.hadm_id 
WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( 
    SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
    FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
    WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'secondary and unspecified malignant neoplasm of intra-abdominal lymph nodes' 
) 
AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100' 
AND prescriptions.drug = 'vial' 
AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime) BETWEEN datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime, '+2 month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'secondary'], ['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'vial'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'secondary and unspecified malignant neoplasm of intra-abdominal lymph nodes' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE prescriptions.drug = 'vial' AND strftime('%Y',prescriptions.starttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T2.starttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime,'+2 month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'secondary'], ['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'vial'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',prescriptions.starttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime']]}

Among patients who were prescribed with potassium chl 40 meq / 1000 ml ns, what are the top four most frequently prescribed drugs at the same time since 1 year ago?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T1.drug FROM ( SELECT prescriptions.drug, COUNT(*) AS frequency FROM prescriptions WHERE prescriptions.hadm_id IN ( SELECT DISTINCT hadm_id FROM prescriptions WHERE prescriptions.drug = 'potassium chl 40 meq / 1000 ml ns' AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime) >= datetime(current_time, '-1 year') ) AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime) >= datetime(current_time, '-1 year') GROUP BY prescriptions.drug ORDER BY frequency DESC LIMIT 4 ) AS T1
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['prescriptions.drug', 't1.drug'], 'tab': ['prescriptions'], 'cond': [['datetime(prescriptions.starttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', '-1 year')"], ['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'potassium chl 40 meq / 1000 ml ns']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE prescriptions.drug = 'potassium chl 40 meq / 1000 ml ns' AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE datetime(prescriptions.starttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE datetime(T1.starttime) = datetime(T2.starttime) GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['datetime(prescriptions.starttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year')"], ['datetime(t1.starttime)', '=', 'datetime(t2.starttime)'], ['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'potassium chl 40 meq / 1000 ml ns'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '4']]}

----------
PREDS:

Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.hadm_id', 't1.drug'], 'tab': ['prescriptions'], 'cond': [['prescriptions.drug', '!=', "lidocaine 0.5%' ) desc"], ['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'lidocaine 0.5%'], ["strftime('%y',prescriptions.starttime)", '>=', '2100']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE prescriptions.drug = 'lidocaine 0.5%' AND strftime('%Y',prescriptions.starttime) >= '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE strftime('%Y',prescriptions.starttime) >= '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE datetime(T1.starttime) = datetime(T2.starttime) GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5

What is the 3-month survival rate for patients diagnosed with other psoriasis?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT AVG(CASE WHEN strftime('%J', patients.dod) - strftime('%J', diagnoses_icd.charttime) < 1 * 365/4 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) AS survival_rate FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN patients ON diagnoses_icd.subject_id = patients.subject_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (SELECT icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE long_title = 'other psoriasis') AND strftime('%J', current_time) - strftime('%J', diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 1 * 365/4
Flattened: {'sel': 'avg', 'agg': ['diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod'], 'tab': ['d_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [["strftime('%j','2100-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '1 * 365/4']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT AVG(C1) FROM ( SELECT ( CASE WHEN (strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',T1.charttime)) < 1 * 365/4 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'other psoriasis' ) AND strftime('%J',current_time) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 1 * 365/4 GROUP BY admissions.subject_id HAVING MIN(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) AS T1 JOIN patients ON T1.subject_id = patients.subject_id )
Flattened: {'sel': 'avg', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 't1.charttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'other psoriasis'], ["strftime('%j','2100-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '1 * 365/4 ) = diagnoses_icd.charttime']]}

What are the four most frequent drugs that were prescribed to male patients in their 30s during the same month after they were diagnosed with hyposmolality and/or hyponatremia during this year?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code IN ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title IN ('hyposmolality and/or hyponatremia') ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y',CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 30 AND 39 AND admissions.gender = 'm' AND strftime('%Y',prescriptions.starttime) = strftime('%Y',CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.starttime,'start of month') GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['admissions.gender', '=', 'm'], ['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', 'in', "('hyposmolality"], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.starttime,'start of month')"], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', "strftime('%y',current_timestamp)"], ["strftime('%y',prescriptions.starttime)", '=', "strftime('%y',current_timestamp)"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '4']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'hyposmolality and/or hyponatremia' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE admissions.subject_id IN ( SELECT patients.subject_id FROM patients WHERE patients.gender = 'm' ) AND admissions.age BETWEEN 30 AND 39 AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.starttime,'start of month') GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.subject_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'hyposmolality'], ["datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ["datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.starttime,'start of month')"], ['patients.gender', '=', 'm'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '4']]}

How many patients died during the same month after having been diagnosed since 1 year ago with acute on chronic diastolic (congestive) heart failure?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT admissions.subject_id) FROM admissions JOIN diagnoses_icd ON admissions.hadm_id = diagnoses_icd.hadm_id JOIN d_icd_diagnoses ON diagnoses_icd.icd_code = d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'acute on chronic diastolic (congestive) heart failure' AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime(current_time, '-1 year') AND datetime(admissions.dischtime, 'start of month') = datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime, 'start of month') AND admissions.dischtime IS NOT NULL AND admissions.subject_id IN (SELECT subject_id FROM patients WHERE patients.dod IS NOT NULL);
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['admissions.dischtime', 'is', 'not null'], ['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'acute on chronic diastolic (congestive) heart failure'], ["datetime(admissions.dischtime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime, 'start of month')"], ['datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', '-1 year')"], ['patients.dod', 'is', 'not null']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T2.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT T1.subject_id, T1.charttime FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'acute on chronic diastolic (congestive) heart failure' ) ) AS T1 GROUP BY T1.subject_id HAVING MIN(T1.charttime) = T1.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) AS T2 JOIN ( SELECT patients.subject_id, admissions.hadm_id, patients.dod FROM admissions join patients ON patients.subject_id = admissions.subject_id WHERE patients.dod IS NOT NULL AND datetime(patients.dod) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) as T3 ON T2.subject_id = T3.subject_id WHERE datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T3.dod,'start of month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 'patients.subject_id', 't1.charttime', 't1.subject_id', 't2.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'acute on chronic diastolic (congestive) heart failure'], ['datetime(patients.dod)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year')"], ["datetime(t2.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t3.dod,'start of month')"], ['patients.dod', 'is', 'not null']]}

What are the four most frequently ordered medications that male patients aged 20s were prescribed within 2 months after they were diagnosed with mitral valve disorders since 1 year ago?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'mitral valve disorders' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') AND admissions.gender = 'm' AND admissions.age BETWEEN 20 AND 29 ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE datetime(prescriptions.starttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T2.starttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime,'+2 month') GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['admissions.gender', '=', 'm'], ['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'mitral valve disorders'], ['datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year')"], ['datetime(prescriptions.starttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '4']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'mitral valve disorders' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE admissions.subject_id IN ( SELECT patients.subject_id FROM patients WHERE patients.gender = 'm' ) AND admissions.age BETWEEN 20 AND 29 AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T2.starttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime,'+2 month') GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.subject_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'mitral valve disorders'], ['datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year')"], ['datetime(prescriptions.starttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year')"], ['patients.gender', '=', 'm'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '4']]}

Count the number of patients who were diagnosed with chronic hepatitis c without mention of hepatic coma but didn't come back to the hospital within the same month.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT T1.subject_id) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'chronic hepatitis c without mention of hepatic coma' ) ) AS T1 LEFT JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, admissions.admittime FROM admissions ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id AND strftime('%Y-%m', T1.charttime) = strftime('%Y-%m', T2.admittime) AND T1.hadm_id != T2.hadm_id WHERE T2.subject_id IS NULL
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.admittime', 'admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'chronic hepatitis c without mention of hepatic coma'], ['t2.subject_id', 'is', 'null']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT ( SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'chronic hepatitis c without mention of hepatic coma' ) ) AS T1 ) - ( SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T2.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'chronic hepatitis c without mention of hepatic coma' ) ) AS T2 JOIN admissions ON T2.subject_id = admissions.subject_id WHERE T2.charttime < admissions.admittime AND datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(admissions.admittime,'start of month') )
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id', 't2.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'chronic hepatitis c without mention of hepatic coma'], ["datetime(t2.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(admissions.admittime,'start of month')"], ['t2.charttime', '<', 'admissions.admittime']]}

List the top four most frequent diagnoses that patients received within 2 months after previously being diagnosed with other candidiasis of other specified sites since 1 year ago.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T2.icd_code, COUNT(*) AS frequency FROM ( SELECT DISTINCT diagnoses_icd.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.hadm_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN d_icd_diagnoses ON diagnoses_icd.icd_code = d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'other candidiasis of other specified sites' AND diagnoses_icd.charttime >= datetime('now', '-1 year') ) AS T1 JOIN diagnoses_icd AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id AND T2.charttime > T1.charttime AND T2.charttime <= datetime(T1.charttime, '+2 months') GROUP BY T2.icd_code ORDER BY frequency DESC LIMIT 4
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'diagnoses_icd.hadm_id', 'diagnoses_icd.subject_id', 't2.icd_code'], 'tab': ['d_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'other candidiasis of other specified sites'], ['diagnoses_icd.charttime', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', '-1 year')"]]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.long_title FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code IN ( SELECT T3.icd_code FROM ( SELECT T2.icd_code, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'other candidiasis of other specified sites' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.icd_code, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T2.charttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime,'+2 month') GROUP BY T2.icd_code ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4 )
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'd_icd_diagnoses.long_title', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'diagnoses_icd.icd_code', 't2.icd_code', 't3.icd_code'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'other candidiasis of other specified sites'], ['datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '4']]}

What is the 3-month survival rate for patients diagnosed with history of falling?
----------
PREDS:
None
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['test."nothing"', '=', 'none']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT AVG(C1) FROM ( SELECT ( CASE WHEN (strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',T1.charttime)) < 1 * 365/4 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'history of falling' ) AND strftime('%J',current_time) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 1 * 365/4 GROUP BY admissions.subject_id HAVING MIN(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) AS T1 JOIN patients ON T1.subject_id = patients.subject_id )
Flattened: {'sel': 'avg', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 't1.charttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'history of falling'], ["strftime('%j','2100-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '1 * 365/4 ) = diagnoses_icd.charttime']]}

How many patients underwent single internal mammary-coronary artery bypass during the same month after the diagnosis with arthropathy, unspecified, lower leg, in 2100?
----------
