What are the top five most frequent medications prescribed within 2 months to the patients in their 50s after they were diagnosed with cerebral artery occlusion, unspecified with cerebral infarction this year?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'cerebral artery occlusion, unspecified with cerebral infarction' ) AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y', 'now') AND admissions.age BETWEEN 50 AND 59 ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE datetime(prescriptions.starttime) >= datetime('now', '-2 months') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'cerebral artery occlusion, unspecified with cerebral infarction'], ['datetime(prescriptions.starttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', '-2 months')"], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', "strftime('%y', '2100-12-31 23:59:00')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'cerebral artery occlusion, unspecified with cerebral infarction' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 50 AND 59 AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T2.starttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime,'+2 month') GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'cerebral artery occlusion, unspecified with cerebral infarction'], ["datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ["datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}

Calculate the total number of patients diagnosed with other and unspecified anti-infectives causing adverse effects in therapeutic use who didn't revisit the hospital within the same month since 2100.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT admissions.subject_id) FROM admissions WHERE admissions.hadm_id IN ( SELECT diagnoses_icd.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'other and unspecified anti-infectives causing adverse effects in therapeutic use' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AND NOT EXISTS ( SELECT 1 FROM admissions AS revisit WHERE revisit.subject_id = admissions.subject_id AND strftime('%Y-%m',revisit.admittime) = strftime('%Y-%m',admissions.dischtime) AND revisit.admittime > admissions.dischtime )
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.hadm_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'other'], ['revisit.admittime', '>', 'admissions.dischtime'], ['revisit.subject_id', '=', 'admissions.subject_id'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y-%m',revisit.admittime)", '=', "strftime('%y-%m',admissions.dischtime)"]]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT ( SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'other and unspecified anti-infectives causing adverse effects in therapeutic use' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T1 ) - ( SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T2.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'other and unspecified anti-infectives causing adverse effects in therapeutic use' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T2 JOIN admissions ON T2.subject_id = admissions.subject_id WHERE T2.charttime < admissions.admittime AND strftime('%Y',admissions.admittime) >= '2100' AND datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(admissions.admittime,'start of month') )
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id', 't2.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'other'], ["datetime(t2.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(admissions.admittime,'start of month')"], ["strftime('%y',admissions.admittime)", '>=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ['t2.charttime', '<', 'admissions.admittime']]}

How many hours has it been since patient 10021487 got their last prescription of glucagon on this hospital stay?
----------
PREDS:

Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'prescriptions.starttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['admissions.subject_id', '=', '10021487'], ['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'glucagon']]}

What are the three most frequent drugs prescribed to female patients with an age of 20s within the same month after they were diagnosed with unspecified acquired hypothyroidism?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'unspecified acquired hypothyroidism' ) AND admissions.gender = 'f' AND admissions.age BETWEEN 20 AND 29 ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE admissions.gender = 'f' AND admissions.age BETWEEN 20 AND 29 ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.starttime,'start of month') AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 3
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['admissions.gender', '=', 'f'], ['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'unspecified acquired hypothyroidism'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.starttime,'start of month')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '3']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'unspecified acquired hypothyroidism' ) ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE admissions.subject_id IN ( SELECT patients.subject_id FROM patients WHERE patients.gender = 'f' ) AND admissions.age BETWEEN 20 AND 29 ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.starttime,'start of month') GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 3

Among patients who were prescribed with phenytoin infatab, what are the top five most frequently prescribed drugs at the same time in 2100?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T1.drug FROM ( SELECT prescriptions.drug, COUNT(*) AS freq FROM prescriptions WHERE prescriptions.hadm_id IN ( SELECT DISTINCT hadm_id FROM prescriptions WHERE prescriptions.drug = 'phenytoin infatab' AND strftime('%Y', prescriptions.starttime) = '2100' ) AND strftime('%Y', prescriptions.starttime) = '2100' AND prescriptions.drug != 'phenytoin infatab' GROUP BY prescriptions.drug ORDER BY freq DESC LIMIT 5 ) AS T1
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['prescriptions.drug', 't1.drug'], 'tab': ['prescriptions'], 'cond': [['prescriptions.drug', '!=', 'phenytoin infatab'], ['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'phenytoin infatab'], ["strftime('%y',prescriptions.starttime)", '=', '2100']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE prescriptions.drug = 'phenytoin infatab' AND strftime('%Y',prescriptions.starttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE strftime('%Y',prescriptions.starttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE datetime(T1.starttime) = datetime(T2.starttime) GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['datetime(t1.starttime)', '=', 'datetime(t2.starttime)'], ['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'phenytoin infatab'], ["strftime('%y',prescriptions.starttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}

Count the number of patients who were diagnosed with irritable bowel syndrome but didn't come back to the hospital within 2 months since 2100.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT T1.subject_id) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'irritable bowel syndrome' ) AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 LEFT JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, admissions.admittime FROM admissions WHERE strftime('%Y', admissions.admittime) = '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id AND datetime(T2.admittime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime, '+2 month') WHERE T2.subject_id IS NULL;
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.admittime', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'irritable bowel syndrome'], ["strftime('%y',admissions.admittime)", '=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t2.subject_id', 'is', 'null']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT ( SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'irritable bowel syndrome' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T1 ) - ( SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T2.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'irritable bowel syndrome' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T2 JOIN admissions ON T2.subject_id = admissions.subject_id WHERE T2.charttime < admissions.admittime AND strftime('%Y',admissions.admittime) >= '2100' AND datetime(admissions.admittime) BETWEEN datetime(T2.charttime) AND datetime(T2.charttime,'+2 month') )
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id', 't2.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'irritable bowel syndrome'], ["strftime('%y',admissions.admittime)", '>=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ['t2.charttime', '<', 'admissions.admittime']]}

SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT T1.subject_id) FROM (SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'thrombocytopenia, unspecified')) AS T1 JOIN (SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'chronic airway obstruction, not elsewhere classified')) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime;

Throughout this year, what are the top five most common drugs prescribed during the same hospital encounter to female patients aged 50s after being diagnosed with epilepsy, unspecified, not intractable, without status epilepticus?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'epilepsy, unspecified, not intractable, without status epilepticus' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 50 AND 59 AND admissions.gender = 'f' AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['admissions.gender', '=', 'f'], ['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'epilepsy, unspecified, not intractable, without status epilepticus'], ["datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year')"], ["datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'epilepsy, unspecified, not intractable, without status epilepticus' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE admissions.subject_id IN ( SELECT patients.subject_id FROM patients WHERE patients.gender = 'f' ) AND admissions.age BETWEEN 50 AND 59 AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.subject_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'epilepsy, unspecified, not intractable, without status epilepticus'], ["datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ["datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ['patients.gender', '=', 'f'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}

How many people died after being diagnosed with posttraumatic stress disorder within 2 months throughout this year?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT admissions.subject_id) FROM admissions JOIN diagnoses_icd ON admissions.hadm_id = diagnoses_icd.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'posttraumatic stress disorder') AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y',CURRENT_DATE) AND admissions.dod IS NOT NULL AND julianday(admissions.dod) - julianday(diagnoses_icd.charttime) <= 60;
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['admissions.dod', 'is', 'not null'], ['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'posttraumatic stress disorder'], ['julianday(admissions.dod) - julianday(diagnoses_icd.charttime)', '<=', '60;'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', "strftime('%y',current_date)"]]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T2.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT T1.subject_id, T1.charttime FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'posttraumatic stress disorder' ) ) AS T1 GROUP BY T1.subject_id HAVING MIN(T1.charttime) = T1.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T2 JOIN ( SELECT patients.subject_id, admissions.hadm_id, patients.dod FROM admissions join patients ON patients.subject_id = admissions.subject_id WHERE patients.dod IS NOT NULL AND datetime(patients.dod,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) as T3 ON T2.subject_id = T3.subject_id WHERE ( datetime(T3.dod) BETWEEN datetime(T2.charttime) AND datetime(T2.charttime,'+2 month') ) OR ( datetime(T2.charttime) BETWEEN datetime(T3.dod) AND datetime(T3.dod,'+2 month') )
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 'patients.subject_id', 't1.charttime', 't1.subject_id', 't2.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'posttraumatic stress disorder'], ["datetime(patients.dod,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ['patients.dod', 'is', 'not null']]}
----------

Find out the daily or urine output volume of patient 10019172 during the previous month. If multiple or urine output volumes are measured daily, take the minimum.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT strftime('%Y-%m-%d', outputevents.charttime) AS day, MIN(outputevents.value) AS min_urine_output FROM outputevents WHERE outputevents.stay_id IN ( SELECT icustays.stay_id FROM icustays WHERE icustays.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10019172 ) ) AND outputevents.itemid IN ( SELECT d_items.itemid FROM d_items WHERE d_items.label = 'or urine' AND d_items.linksto = 'outputevents' ) AND datetime(outputevents.charttime) >= datetime(current_time, 'start of month', '-1 month') AND datetime(outputevents.charttime) < datetime(current_time, 'start of month') GROUP BY strftime('%Y-%m-%d', outputevents.charttime)
Flattened: {'sel': 'min', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'd_items.itemid', 'icustays.stay_id', 'outputevents.charttime', 'outputevents.value'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_items', 'icustays', 'outputevents'], 'cond': [['admissions.subject_id', '=', '10019172'], ['d_items.label', '=', 'or urine'], ['d_items.linksto', '=', 'outputevents'], ['datetime(outputevents.charttime)', '<', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', 'start of month') )"], ['datetime(outputevents.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', 'start of month', '-1 month')"]]}
----------

What are the five most frequently prescribed medications for patients diagnosed with urethral stricture, unspecified previously within 2 months, this year?
----------
PREDS:

Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'urethral stricture, unspecified'], ['diagnoses_icd.charttime', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', '-2 months')"], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', "strftime('%y','2100-12-31 23:59:00')"], ["strftime('%y',prescriptions.starttime)", '=', "strftime('%y','2100-12-31 23:59:00')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}
----------
TRUTH:

What is the 3-month survival rate for patients diagnosed with pleural effusion, not elsewhere classified.
----------
PREDS:
None
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['test."nothing"', '=', 'none']]}
----------
TRUTH:

Flattened: {'sel': 'avg', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 't1.charttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'pleural effusion, not elsewhere classified'], ["strftime('%j','2100-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '1 * 365/4 ) = diagnoses_icd.charttime']]}

How many people died after being diagnosed with long term (current) use of opiate analgesic during the same month during the last year?
----------
PREDS:
None
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['test."nothing"', '=', 'none']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T2.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT T1.subject_id, T1.charttime FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'long term (current) use of opiate analgesic' ) ) AS T1 GROUP BY T1.subject_id HAVING MIN(T1.charttime) = T1.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-1 year') ) AS T2 JOIN ( SELECT patients.subject_id, admissions.hadm_id, patients.dod FROM admissions join patients ON patients.subject_id = admissions.subject_id WHERE patients.dod IS NOT NULL AND datetime(patients.dod,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-1 year') ) as T3 ON T2.subject_id = T3.subject_id WHERE datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T3.dod,'start of month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 'patients.subject_id', 't1.charttime', 't1.subject_id', 't2.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'long term (current) use of opiate analgesic'], ["datetime(patients.dod,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-1 year')"], ["datetime(t2.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t3.dod,'start of month')"], ['patients.dod', 'is', 'not null']]}

What is the number of patients who died after diagnosis of septic shock within 2 months during the last year?
----------
PREDS:
None
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['test."nothing"', '=', 'none']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T2.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT T1.subject_id, T1.charttime FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'septic shock' ) ) AS T1 GROUP BY T1.subject_id HAVING MIN(T1.charttime) = T1.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-1 year') ) AS T2 JOIN ( SELECT patients.subject_id, admissions.hadm_id, patients.dod FROM admissions join patients ON patients.subject_id = admissions.subject_id WHERE patients.dod IS NOT NULL AND datetime(patients.dod,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-1 year') ) as T3 ON T2.subject_id = T3.subject_id WHERE ( datetime(T3.dod) BETWEEN datetime(T2.charttime) AND datetime(T2.charttime,'+2 month') ) OR ( datetime(T2.charttime) BETWEEN datetime(T3.dod) AND datetime(T3.dod,'+2 month') )
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 'patients.subject_id', 't1.charttime', 't1.subject_id', 't2.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'septic shock'], ["datetime(patients.dod,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-1 year')"], ['patients.dod', 'is', 'not null']]}

PRED: SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT T1.subject_id) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'chronic hepatitis c without mention of hepatic coma' ) ) AS T1 LEFT JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, admissions.admittime FROM admissions ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id AND strftime('%Y-%m', T1.charttime) = strftime('%Y-%m', T2.admittime) AND T1.hadm_id != T2.hadm_id WHERE T2.subject_id IS NULL
GOLD: SELECT ( SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'chronic hepatitis c without mention of hepatic coma' ) ) AS T1 ) - ( SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T2.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'chronic hepatitis c without mention of hepatic coma' ) ) AS T2 JOIN admissions ON T2.subject_id = admissions.subject_id WHERE T2.charttime < admissions.admittime AND datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(admissions.admittime,'start of month') )


Give me the top four most frequent diagnoses that patients were diagnosed with in the same month after being diagnosed with body mass index 35.0-35.9, adult this year.
PRED: SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.long_title FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code IN ( SELECT T1.icd_code FROM ( SELECT diagnoses_icd.icd_code, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM diagnoses_icd WHERE diagnoses_icd.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.hadm_id IN ( SELECT diagnoses_icd.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'body mass index (bmi) 35.0-35.9, adult' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y','2100-12-31 23:59:00'stamp) ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(( SELECT diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'body mass index (bmi) 35.0-35.9, adult' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y','2100-12-31 23:59:00'stamp) ),'start of month') ) GROUP BY diagnoses_icd.icd_code ) AS T1 WHERE T1.C1 <= 4 )
GOLD: SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.long_title FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code IN ( SELECT T3.icd_code FROM ( SELECT T2.icd_code, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'body mass index 35.0-35.9, adult' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year') = datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.icd_code, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year') = datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month') GROUP BY T2.icd_code ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4 )

List the top four most frequent diagnoses that patients received within 2 months after previously being diagnosed with other candidiasis of other specified sites since 1 year ago.
PRED: SELECT T2.icd_code, COUNT(*) AS frequency FROM ( SELECT DISTINCT diagnoses_icd.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.hadm_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN d_icd_diagnoses ON diagnoses_icd.icd_code = d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'other candidiasis of other specified sites' AND diagnoses_icd.charttime >= datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', '-1 year') ) AS T1 JOIN diagnoses_icd AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id AND T2.charttime > T1.charttime AND T2.charttime <= datetime(T1.charttime, '+2 months') GROUP BY T2.icd_code ORDER BY frequency DESC LIMIT 4
GOLD: SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.long_title FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code IN ( SELECT T3.icd_code FROM ( SELECT T2.icd_code, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'other candidiasis of other specified sites' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.icd_code, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T2.charttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime,'+2 month') GROUP BY T2.icd_code ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4 )
PRED_OUT: Counter()
GOLD_OUT: Counter({('other and unspecified mycoses',): 1, ('secondary diabetes mellitus without mention of complication, not stated as uncontrolled, or unspecified',): 1, ('pure hypercholesterolemia',): 1, ('anemia, unspecified',): 1, ('alcohol abuse, unspecified',): 1, ('unspecified essential hypertension',): 1, ('acute pancreatitis',): 1, ('cyst and pseudocyst of pancreas',): 1, ('other specified diseases of pancreas',): 1, ('personal history of malignant neoplasm of breast',): 1, ('acquired absence of breast and nipple',): 1, ('personal history of antineoplastic chemotherapy',): 1})


What is the 3-month survival rate for patients diagnosed with history of falling?
PRED: None
GOLD: SELECT AVG(C1) FROM ( SELECT ( CASE WHEN (strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',T1.charttime)) < 1 * 365/4 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'history of falling' ) AND strftime('%J','2100-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 1 * 365/4 GROUP BY admissions.subject_id HAVING MIN(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) AS T1 JOIN patients ON T1.subject_id = patients.subject_id )


For patients who underwent (aorto)coronary bypass of three coronary arteries in 2100, what were the top four most frequent procedures that followed within the same hospital visit?
PRED: SELECT T3.long_title FROM ( SELECT T2.icd_code, COUNT(*) AS procedure_count FROM ( SELECT procedures_icd.hadm_id, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = '(aorto)coronary bypass of three coronary arteries' ) AND strftime('%Y', procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT procedures_icd.hadm_id, procedures_icd.icd_code, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd ) AS T2 ON T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime GROUP BY T2.icd_code ORDER BY procedure_count DESC LIMIT 4 ) AS T3 JOIN d_icd_procedures ON T3.icd_code = d_icd_procedures.icd_code
GOLD: SELECT d_icd_procedures.long_title FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.icd_code IN ( SELECT T3.icd_code FROM ( SELECT T2.icd_code, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = '(aorto)coronary bypass of three coronary arteries' ) AND strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.icd_code, procedures_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id GROUP BY T2.icd_code ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4 )


How many patients died after having been diagnosed with facial weakness during the same hospital encounter throughout this year?
PRED: SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT admissions.subject_id) FROM admissions JOIN diagnoses_icd ON admissions.hadm_id = diagnoses_icd.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'facial weakness') AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y', '2100-12-31 23:59:00') AND admissions.dod IS NOT NULL AND strftime('%Y', admissions.admittime) = strftime('%Y', '2100-12-31 23:59:00');
GOLD: SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T2.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT T1.subject_id, T1.charttime, T1.hadm_id FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'facial weakness' ) ) AS T1 GROUP BY T1.subject_id HAVING MIN(T1.charttime) = T1.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of year') = datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year') ) AS T2 JOIN ( SELECT patients.subject_id, admissions.hadm_id, patients.dod FROM admissions join patients ON patients.subject_id = admissions.subject_id WHERE patients.dod IS NOT NULL AND datetime(patients.dod,'start of year') = datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year') ) as T3 ON T2.subject_id = T3.subject_id WHERE T2.hadm_id = T3.hadm_id

PRED: SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT admissions.subject_id) FROM admissions WHERE admissions.hadm_id IN ( SELECT diagnoses_icd.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'other and unspecified anti-infectives causing adverse effects in therapeutic use' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AND NOT EXISTS ( SELECT 1 FROM admissions AS revisit WHERE revisit.subject_id = admissions.subject_id AND strftime('%Y-%m',revisit.admittime) = strftime('%Y-%m',admissions.dischtime) AND revisit.admittime > admissions.dischtime )
GOLD: SELECT ( SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'other and unspecified anti-infectives causing adverse effects in therapeutic use' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T1 ) - ( SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T2.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'other and unspecified anti-infectives causing adverse effects in therapeutic use' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T2 JOIN admissions ON T2.subject_id = admissions.subject_id WHERE T2.charttime < admissions.admittime AND strftime('%Y',admissions.admittime) >= '2100' AND datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(admissions.admittime,'start of month') )

GOLD_OUT: Counter({(1,): 1})

PRED: SELECT strftime('%m', prescriptions.starttime) AS month, COUNT(*) AS prescription_count FROM prescriptions WHERE prescriptions.drug LIKE '%antihistamine%' GROUP BY month ORDER BY prescription_count DESC LIMIT 1

How many admissions had a last contact time on a weekend and were discharged to home?
